- **05:** a purple slotted tunnel with gold ring bands and gold paddle lids. A wide cyan river with hot-pink bands winds in S-curves into a bright far end, with foam banks, a centre coin line and a white splash around Benny.
- **06:** the river rises in a crest, sweeps left, then bends right into an off-centre gold-ring tunnel, not a straight bullseye. Paddle lids at the upper left, coins along the crest, ripple rings around Benny.
- **07:** a deep purple slotted tube with many gold rings and paddles on the left. A winding cyan river with thick magenta edges plunges away, with foam clouds and coins.
- **08:** a wide chamber framed by a huge gold ring, with the washer door and porthole at the far end over a platform with a folded purple towel. Four spout cups pour from their undersides, and an S-river with coins winds through purple perforated walls.

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-18T14:51:52.230Z

Three real-game results are ready for you. All three are improvements, but none of the three screens is close enough to approve yet.

**Screen 04, step 2: the five arrows while swimming — GOOD IMPROVEMENT (the arrows only)**
- **Closer:** before, the glowing arrows disappeared as soon as Benny started swimming. Now a stack of white-cyan chevrons points down the lane above his hat, going straight and at hard left and right, as in your picture. None of them cut through Benny, including at the end of the screen.
- **Unchanged:** the start view looks the same, by eye.
- **Still wrong:** the chevrons are paler and more see-through than your bright neon arrows.
- **The two biggest differences now:**
  - The tunnel is a dense corridor of thin orange rings. Yours has fewer, thicker, polished yellow-gold rings with light dots.
  - The water sides repeat rows of parallel foam ridges.
- **Clip:** the 2.2-second clip is real gameplay. That is the whole of Screen 04 at game speed, swimming straight, then left, then right.

**Screen 05, step 2: one continuous river — GOOD IMPROVEMENT**
- **Closer:** about two-thirds and four-fifths of the way through, the pale slab and the straight line across the far river are gone. The pink bands and cyan water now run unbroken into the far tunnel.
- **Next:** foam patches cut by a straight edge, and the flat pale-lilac left bank in the distance.
- **Rejected:** the specialist's first test, the unfinished wall-slot fix from the previous account, was NOT AN IMPROVEMENT. It left doubled "ghost" slots near the walls when steering, so it was reverted.

**Screen 08, step 1: the big gold ring's top-left edge — GOOD IMPROVEMENT, small**
- **Closer:** while swimming, the ring now curves smoothly there. Before, a hard straight diagonal edge and a strip of purple slots cut into it.
- **Next:** the gold wedge on top of the upper-right spout cup.

The pictures are saved in `qa/review_r13/` and backed up to GitHub (`a4057e47`). Both specialists keep working while I rebuild the Screen 04 rings and water sides.

Please still answer my earlier question: did I understand your visual goal correctly, and do you approve the visual-first plan?

### 🤖 Claude — 2026-11-18T15:16:46.427Z

Screen 04 now reads the way you described: gold tunnel, clean blue centre path, and white arrows ahead of Benny. The full-size APPROVED / BEFORE / NEW pictures are above (straight, hard left, hard right, plus the end of the screen), with a 2.2-second real-gameplay swim.

**Verdict: GOOD IMPROVEMENT, but not yet at your 8.5/10.**

**What is closer:**
- **Side water:** pushed outward and lowered to under half its old height. The foam walls and lumpy foam ridges are gone, and pink stays only at the outer edges.
- **Centre path:** one smooth, continuous blue lane, easy to read from Benny to the far end.
- **Arrows:** solid white, not see-through. Four or five show ahead of Benny going straight and while steering left or right.
- **Gold tunnel:** much more of it shows. The rings are fewer and broader, with dark grooves between them, instead of the cluttered thin orange corridor.
- **Unchanged:** Benny, the gameplay view, coins and the start view.

**What is still wrong:**
- The blue path is paler and flatter than your saturated cyan with sparkles.
- The rings are still more orange than your copper-gold with pink reflections.
- At hard left, the nearest arrow ends just beside Benny's hat brim. It sits behind him, not over him.
- A passing bubble can cover one arrow for a moment.

Next on Screen 04 I'll make the blue path more saturated and sparkly, and push the ring colour toward your copper-gold.

The specialists have reported two more kept steps, which I'll check by eye before showing you:
- **Screen 05:** the flat pale floor and lilac wedge on the far left bank are replaced by purple wall and foam clouds.
- **Screen 08:** the upper-right spout cup's top is now a pink perforated cap instead of a gold triangle with hard X lines.

Everything is saved to GitHub, which matches the local copy: Screen 04 branch at `4544d5c7`, main session branch at `4e52a4f9`. The five-minute autosave is still running.

1. MAKE THE BLUE WATER LOOK LIKE MY APPROVED ARTWORK

Your current water is too pale, smooth and somewhat flat.

Please:

- Increase the richness and saturation of the blue and cyan water.
- Restore glossy, flowing highlights, sparkles and natural-looking swirls.
- Give the water more depth and dimensional appearance.
- Keep the center path wide, continuous and clearly visible.
- Preserve the natural swimming water around Benny, including his splashes.
- Make sure the water remains attractive while Benny swims forward and steers.

DO NOT turn the water into a flat blue road or solid-colored platform.

The blue should look like flowing magical water, not painted plastic.

2. CORRECT THE GOLD TUNNEL MATERIALS

My approved tunnel has polished copper-gold metallic rings with deeper golden shadows, dark recessed grooves and bright reflected highlights.

The current tunnel still looks too orange.

Please:

- Shift the ring materials toward rich copper-gold rather than flat orange.
- Add darker recessed grooves and dimensional shading.
- Strengthen the polished metallic highlights.
- Include subtle pink, purple and warm-gold reflections consistent with my approved picture.
- Preserve the broader ring spacing and shapes already achieved.
- Keep the gold architecture recognizable throughout swimming and steering.

The tunnel must look like expensive, polished metallic gold, not a flat orange corridor.

3. SOFTEN THE WATER EDGES WITHOUT RECREATING THE OLD FOAM WALLS

The new low-sided water is an improvement, but some edges form hard triangular boundaries against the gold tunnel.

Please:

- Make the water-to-tunnel transitions softer and more natural.
- Keep pink and magenta currents primarily along the outside edges.
- Add small, irregular foam accents only where the approved artwork shows them.
- Avoid repeated lumpy ridges, tall water walls and bulky foam piles.
- Keep the central blue path visually dominant.

Do not undo the cleaner composition we just achieved.

4. PRESERVE THE FIVE GLOWING ARROWS AND BENNY

5. COMPARE ACTUAL GAMEPLAY, NOT JUST A STILL PICTURE

Compare the entire scene against my original approved artwork, especially the color, materials, water appearance and tunnel proportions.

If a new experiment looks worse, reject it and preserve the previous version.

Do not claim a visual improvement based only on technical measurements.

Continue the existing Screen 05 and Screen 08 specialists without interruption.

Preserve their unfinished work and successful improvements.

Maintain five-minute automatic GitHub autosave and verify successful pushes.

My priority remains the visual reconstruction of ALL FIVE playable approved screens, 04–08.

7. CONFIRM THE NEXT VISUAL TASK

Please reply briefly confirming:

1. You understand that the blue water needs richer saturation, natural glossy movement and more sparkle — without becoming flat or crowded again.
2. You understand that the gold rings need deeper copper-gold materials and realistic metallic reflections.
3. You will soften the hard water edges while preserving the clean blue center path.
4. You will preserve the successful C5 arrows and Benny.
5. You will show real-game APPROVED / BEFORE / NEW comparisons before declaring success.

I APPROVE THESE SCREEN 04 VISUAL CORRECTIONS. PROCEED WITHOUT REPEATING SETUP OR ASKING ME FOR THE SAME PERMISSION AGAIN.

My final goal is to make Screen 04, and eventually all five playable screens, look as close as technically possible to my original approved artwork while Benny swims naturally through the real 3D game.

VISUALS FIRST. PRESERVE SUCCESSFUL WORK. NO REDESIGNS.
